-- discord.gg/syncrypt

local function geniter(f, s, c)
	if type(f) ~= "function" then
		local mt = getmetatable(f)

		if type(mt) == "table" and mt.__iter then
			f, s, c = mt.__iter(f)
		elseif type(f) == "table" then
			f, s, c = next, f, nil
		end
	end

	return function()
		local r = table.pack(f(s, c))

		if r[1] == nil then
			return false
		end

		c = r[1]

		return true, table.unpack(r, 1, r.n)
	end
end

local function v20(p1, p2)
	local v21 = Players:FindFirstChild(p1)

	if not v21 then
		return false
	end

	if not p2[p1] then
		return false
	end

	local UserId = v21.UserId

	if not t5[p1] then
		t5[p1] = UserId

		return true
	end

	if t5[p1] == UserId then
		return true
	end

	if v21 == LocalPlayer then
		StarterGui:SetCore("ChatMakeSystemMessage", {
			Text = "[Coquette Hub] ⚠️ ACESSO NEGADO: Detecção de burla de identidade!",
			Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),
			Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
		})
	end

	return false
end
local function v23()
	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		local Name = player.Name

		if t2[Name] or t3[Name] or t4[Name] then
			local v27

			if t2[Name] then
				v27 = t2
			elseif t3[Name] then
				v27 = t3
			elseif t4[Name] then
				v27 = t4
			end

			if v27 then
				v20(Name, v27)
			end
		end
	end
end

local function v30()
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local lib = loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/vitorhub457/Kshslsusbejdbs/refs/heads/main/Drgferffnaks"))()

		if type(lib) ~= "table" then
			return nil
		end

		for k, v in pairs(lib) do
			if type(k) ~= "string" or type(v) ~= "boolean" then
				return nil
			end
		end

		return lib
	end)

	if ok and result then
		return result
	end

	return {}
end

local function v51(p10)
	local now = tick()

	if not t1[p10] then
		t1[p10] = {
			lastCommand = 0,
			commandCount = 0,
			resetTime = now
		}

		return true
	end

	local v53 = t1[p10]

	if now - v53.resetTime >= 60 then
		v53.commandCount = 0
		v53.resetTime = now
	end

	if now - v53.lastCommand < 10 then
		return false, "Rate limit: aguarde " .. math.ceil(10 - (now - v53.lastCommand)) .. " segundos"
	end

	if v53.commandCount >= 3 then
		return false, "Rate limit: máximo 3 comandos por minuto"
	end

	v53.lastCommand = now
	v53.commandCount += 1

	return true
end

		elseif state <= 58 then
			if state == 57 then
				if maxDistance2 < Magnitude2 then
					state = 58
				else
					state = 59
				end
			elseif state == 58 then
				HumanoidRootPart2.CFrame = CFrame.new(v142.jailCenter)
				HumanoidRootPart2.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				HumanoidRootPart2.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				state = 59
			end
		elseif state <= 60 then
			if state == 59 then
				if v115.Character then
					state = 60
				else
					state = 48
				end
			elseif state == 60 then
				state = 41
			end
		elseif state <= 62 then
			if state == 61 then
				if v137:IsA("Tool") then
					state = 62
				else
					state = 45
				end
			elseif state == 62 then
				if v137.Name:lower():find("fly") then
					state = 66
				else
					state = 63
				end
			end
		elseif state <= 64 then
			if state == 63 then
				if v137.Name:lower():find("noclip") then
					state = 66
				else
					state = 64
				end
			elseif state == 64 then
				if v137.Name:lower():find("teleport") then
					state = 66
				else
					state = 65
				end
			end
		elseif state <= 66 then
			if state == 65 then
				if v137.Name:lower():find("speed") then
					state = 66
				else
					state = 45
				end
			elseif state == 66 then
				v137:Destroy()
				state = 45
			end
		elseif state <= 68 then
			if state == 67 then
				if v122:IsA("Tool") then
					state = 68
				else
					state = 41
				end
			elseif state == 68 then
				if v122.Name:lower():find("fly") then
					state = 39
				else
					state = 69
				end
			end
		elseif state == 69 then
			v130 = v122.Name:lower()
			state = 36
		end
	end
end)

local function v214(p16)
	local v215 = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Jail_" .. p16.Name)

	if v215 then
		pcall(function()
			v215:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	for _, descendant in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") and descendant.Name == "JailPart_" .. p16.Name then
			pcall(function()
				descendant:Destroy()
			end)
		end
	end

	if p16 then
		if t23[p16.Name] then
			local v218 = t23[p16.Name]

			if v218.loop then
				pcall(function()
					task.cancel(v218.loop)
				end)
			end

			if v218.charConnection then
				pcall(function()
					v218.charConnection:Disconnect()
				end)
			end

			if v218.heartbeatConnection then
				pcall(function()
					v218.heartbeatConnection:Disconnect()
				end)
			end

			if v218.jailModel and v218.jailModel.Parent then
				pcall(function()
					v218.jailModel:Destroy()
				end)
			end

			if p16.Character then
				local Humanoid = p16.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

				if Humanoid then
					Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
					Humanoid.JumpPower = 50
					Humanoid.JumpHeight = 7.2
					Humanoid.PlatformStand = false
				end

				local HumanoidRootPart = p16.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

				if HumanoidRootPart then
					for _, child in ipairs(HumanoidRootPart:GetChildren()) do
						if not child:IsA("BodyVelocity") and not child:IsA("BodyPosition") and not child:IsA("BodyThrust") and not child:IsA("BodyForce") then
							continue
						end

						pcall(function()
							child:Destroy()
						end)
					end

					HumanoidRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
					HumanoidRootPart.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				end
			end

			t23[p16.Name] = nil

			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

local function v573(p40)
	if p40 then
		if p40 ~= "" then
			local v574 = p40:lower():gsub("%s+", "")

			for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				if player.Name:lower():find(v574, 1, true) then
					return player
				end

				if player.DisplayName:lower():find(v574, 1, true) then
					return player
				end
			end

			return nil
		end
	end

	return nil
end
local function v577(p41)
	if not p41 or p41 == "" then
		return nil
	end

	local v578 = string.lower(p41)

	for _, player in pairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if string.lower(player.Name) == v578 then
			return player
		end
	end

	for _, player in pairs(game.Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if string.find(string.lower(player.Name), v578, 1, true) then
			return player
		end
	end

	return nil
end
local function createBillboardGui(p42, p43, p44)
	local BillboardGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")

	BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 180, 0, 30)
	BillboardGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
	BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true

	local Frame = Instance.new("Frame", BillboardGui)

	Frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
	Instance.new("UICorner", Frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)

	local TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", Frame)

	TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	TextLabel.Text = p42
	TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	TextLabel.TextScaled = true
	TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
	TextLabel.TextColor3 = p43
	coroutine.wrap(function()
		while BillboardGui.Parent do
			TextLabel.TextColor3 = p43
			task.wait(0.5)
			TextLabel.TextColor3 = p44
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)()

	return BillboardGui
end

	if not p45 then
		return false, "Jogador não encontrado"
	end

	end

		local v617

				v617 = v
				exitTo = 1

				break
			end
		end

		local wait, n7

		if exitTo == 1 then
			local v622 = Wear
			local InvokeServer = Wear.InvokeServer
			local tonumber_ = tonumber

			while true do

				task.wait(0.2)

				local exitTo2

				while true do
					local v626 = table.pack(v615())

					if v626[1] then
						v617 = v626[3]

						if v617.AssetId then
							exitTo2 = 1

							break
						end
					else
						break
					end
				end

				if exitTo2 ~= 1 then
					break
				end

				v622 = Wear
				InvokeServer = Wear.InvokeServer
				tonumber_ = tonumber
			end

			ChangeCharacterBody:InvokeServer(unpack({ {
				0,
				0,
				0,
				0,
				0,
				0
			} }))
			task.wait(0.3)

			task.wait(0.3)
			Wear:InvokeServer(3164473649)
			task.wait(0.3)
			ChangeBodyColor:FireServer("Really red")
			task.wait(0.3)
			Wear:InvokeServer(6093233760)

		else
			ChangeCharacterBody:InvokeServer(unpack({ {
				0,

			} }))
			task.wait(0.3)

			task.wait(0.3)

		end

		wait(n7)

		return true
	end)

			Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0),

	end

	return false

local function v1169()
	local t80 = {}

	local function v1171(child)
		if child then
			if not t80[child] then
				t80[child] = true
				child.MessageReceived:Connect(function(p85)
					local TextSource = p85.TextSource

					if not TextSource then
						return
					end

					local playerByUserId = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(TextSource.UserId)

					if playerByUserId then
						v1004(playerByUserId, p85.Text)
					end
				end)

				return
			end
		end
	end

	local TextChannels = TextChatService:WaitForChild("TextChannels")

	for _, child in ipairs(TextChannels:GetChildren()) do
		v1171(child)
	end

	TextChannels.ChildAdded:Connect(v1171)
end

v1169()
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
	v214(player)
	v160(player)
	v502(player.Character)
	v282(player)
	t6[player.Name] = nil
	t7[player.Name] = nil

	if player.Character then
		if player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
			local FloatVelocity = player.Character.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("FloatVelocity")

			if FloatVelocity then
				FloatVelocity:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end

	for i = 1, 5 do
		local v1179 = Workspace:FindFirstChild("KillPlusPart_" .. player.Name .. "_" .. i)

		if v1179 then
			v1179:Destroy()
		end
	end
end)

local function v1263(p86)
	if not Players:FindFirstChild(p86) then
		return false
	end

	if not (v41(p86) or v42(p86) or v43(p86) or v48(p86)) then
		return false
	end

	if t2[p86] then
		return v20(p86, t2)
	end

	if t3[p86] then
		return v20(p86, t3)
	end

	if t4[p86] then
		return v20(p86, t4)
	end

	return true
end

local n17 = 0

local function v1272()
	local now = tick()

	if now - n17 > 1 then
		t82 = {}

		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			table.insert(t82, player.Name)
		end

		table.sort(t82)
		n17 = now
	end

	return t82
end

				end

			end
		end)
	end)
end

				ok = not u1268.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			end

			if not ok then
				if CurrentCamera then
					CameraType = CurrentCamera.CameraType
					CameraSubject = CurrentCamera.CameraSubject

					CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = u1268.Character.HumanoidRootPart
				end

				if connection then
					connection:Disconnect()
				end

				connection = u1268.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
					if character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and CurrentCamera then
						CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = character.HumanoidRootPart
					end
				end)
			elseif type(v1282) == "table" and v1282.Set then
				v1282:Set(false)
			elseif type(v1282) == "function" then
				v1282(false)
			end
		elseif connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
			connection = nil
			CurrentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

			if CurrentCamera and CameraType and CameraSubject then
				CurrentCamera.CameraType = CameraType
				CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = CameraSubject
			elseif CurrentCamera and LocalPlayer.Character then
				if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
					CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
					CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid
				end
			end
		else
			CurrentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

				CurrentCamera.CameraType = CameraType
				CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = CameraSubject

				return
			elseif CurrentCamera and LocalPlayer.Character then
				if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
					CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
					CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid

					return
				end
			end
		end
	end
